# OLLAMA: detailed extraction and requirement relevance
- Number of entries in this source snapshot: **511**
- Categories: **23**
- What this source actually provides: Local and cloud model engine plus APIs. Community integrations are third-party projects. The model catalog contains families, not 268 locally guaranteed runnable models; check each model hardware, edition and license separately.
- Scope: all listed items are retained, including low-priority, historical, and third-party ones. No new test run was conducted.
- See `filtered_high_relevance.md` for the useful shortlist and `catalog.csv` for spreadsheet-style search.

- Usage type: Model selection option, not code or agent runtime
- Historical requirements connection: 11: Local models and independence, 12: Model selection and multi-provider comparison
- Why it may matter: Possible model family to review for a future capability. The family name alone is not proof of a specific capability, memory fit or license.
